69e60b59a59dc638da38f1deeb3799b9e3d2a7f0 [ diff ] 
Net: Remove unused chrome_browser_net::Referrer

`chrome/browser/net/referrer.{h,cc}` (`ReferrerValue`, `SubresourceMap`
and `Referrer`) are leftovers from the long-removed
`chrome_browser_net::Predictor`. Nothing in Chromium instantiates or
references these classes anymore; the only remaining `#include`s were
stale ones in `sad_tab.cc`, `usb_chooser_controller.cc` and
`web_usb_detector.cc`, which do not use any of its symbols.

Delete the files, drop them from `chrome/browser/net/BUILD.gn`, and
remove the stale includes.

This CL was prepared with the help of an AI agent (Kaizen Chrome); the
author reviewed and verified it.

Bug: None
Test: CQ Dry Run
Kaizen-Agent: kaizen-chrome

[iOS] Add experimental setting to reset Magic Stack Tips state

Testing the natural Magic Stack Tips V2 priority train (impression caps,
interaction suppression, and eligibility signals) currently requires
deleting and reinstalling the app once a tip has been interacted with or
exhausted its 3-impression limit.
This CL adds a "Reset Magic Stack Tips State" toggle in Experimental
Settings so testers can reset all Magic Stack Tips impression counters,
interaction flags, signal history, and notification permissions on
startup without reinstalling Chrome or losing their current NTP
background configuration. Once triggered on launch, the toggle
automatically resets itself back to off so subsequent app launches
behave normally for multi-launch impression testing.

85345048d28eebcab7b13d3dc0a9b5d7893d320b [ diff ] 
[Linux] Enable the GlobalShortcuts portal on GNOME

The GlobalShortcuts portal was disabled on GNOME because
xdg-desktop-portal-gnome returns an empty list from ListShortcuts, which
caused the shortcut dialog to be shown on every browser start. Since
the listener now binds a complete, persisted set of shortcuts that
GNOME accepts without a dialog, the workaround is no longer needed.

Enable the portal on GNOME and set preferred triggers by default, as on
other desktops. kGlobalShortcutsPortalPreferredTrigger can still
override the latter.

[DTC][Android] Read device signals consent from SigninPrefs

On Android, the enterprise signals disclaimer stores the user's consent
per account in SigninPrefs, keyed by the primary account's Gaia ID. The
profile-level kDeviceSignalsConsentReceived and
kDeviceSignalsPermanentConsentReceived prefs are never written on
Android, so UserPermissionServiceImpl::HasUserConsented() always
returned false there.

This CL makes HasUserConsented() read
SigninPrefs::GetDeviceSignalsPermanentConsentReceived() for the primary
account on Android. To support this, UserDelegate gets an Android-only
GetPrimaryAccountGaiaId(), implemented in UserDelegateImpl and
MockUserDelegate. It returns an empty GaiaId when there is no primary
account or no IdentityManager. In that case consent is treated as
missing, which also avoids the SigninPrefs CHECK on an empty Gaia ID.
That matters because the DTC navigation throttle calls
ShouldCollectConsent() on every navigation, including for signed-out
users.

The two profile prefs are no longer registered on Android, and
kDeviceSignalsConsentReceived is compiled out there. No pref deprecation
is needed because nothing on Android ever wrote them.
kDeviceSignalsPermanentConsentReceived stays defined on Android because
its string is reused as the SigninPrefs key.

This affects the DTC consumers of HasUserConsented():
ShouldCollectConsent(), CanCollectSignals() and
chrome://connectors-internals. Signal Sharing is unchanged. It will be
handled in a separate CL.

The temporary IsCollectSignalsConsentRequired() bypass for managed
profiles on Android is intentionally kept. It will be removed in a
follow-up CL once the disclaimer is rolled out.

Desktop, iOS and ChromeOS behavior is unchanged.

[ios][deflake] Wait for regular tab grid in IncognitoReauthTestCase

testBackgroundAndForegroundFromIncognitoPageShouldShowBlockUI asserted
that the regular tab grid was sufficiently visible right after tapping
the Open Tabs panel button. The tab grid scrolls to the regular panel
with an animation that EarlGrey doesn't wait for, so the assertion often
ran mid-scroll and failed (e.g. 57% visible) on iOS 27.2.

Wait for the regular tab grid to become sufficiently visible instead.

[gap-decorations] Fix flex rule with space-between

Flex gap decorations place each column rule at the midpoint of the
effective gap, which is the `gap` plus the space that `justify-content`
inserts between items.

For `space-around` and `space-evenly`, these values are equal, so the
rule was correct. However, I found a bug for `space-between` where with
exactly two items in a line, there is no space after the last item, so
the effective gap was only the `gap` value. As a result, the rule was
painted next to the second item instead of at the center of the gap.

This CL records the effective gap size from the space after the first
item, which is the space between the first two items.

This CL adds flex-gap-decorations-070 to cover the three distribution
values.

da119b3938321d1f982c93175525a21251a69af0 [ diff ] 
Cache Windows memory pressure commit histograms

Windows memory pressure sampling records three commit metrics on the
browser UI thread. The function-based UMA calls look up each histogram
on every sample, which can block on the StatisticsRecorder global lock.

The three calls now use cached UMA macros, avoiding repeated lookups
after initialization. Metric names, buckets and sampling behavior are
unchanged. The added test checks repeated samples.

Bug: 578297813
Test: Windows x64 Release memory-pressure tests (43 tests, 20 repeats)

tree
775862f6151e7662200c5c9a1226539750260ce7
parent
7ee335610a61ef3137e9f8b4e27b7056fd4aee92 [ diff ] 
[Linux] Forget GlobalShortcuts portal shortcuts of uninstalled extensions

GlobalAcceleratorListenerLinux persists the global shortcuts it has
offered to the portal, and binds them even when their owner isn't
loaded. Shortcuts of uninstalled extensions, and of extensions whose
commands were all removed by an update, were never removed.

Add GlobalAcceleratorListener::OnAcceleratorGroupRemoved(), and call it
from ExtensionCommandsGlobalRegistry when an extension is uninstalled or
loaded without commands, so that its shortcuts are no longer bound and
are dropped from the portal with the next bind. The session is closed
if nothing is left to bind. Shortcuts of disabled extensions are kept so
that re-enabling them doesn't show the dialog again.

Portals like KDE keep listing stored shortcuts until a bind without
them succeeds, so removed shortcuts are remembered as removed until
then, or until ListShortcuts stops reporting them. Otherwise they would
be passed through as untracked shortcuts and bound again.

📋 Metadata

5095ba637c07933450c691128fbc50f5b910280d
[ log ]
author
Camille Lamy <clamy@chromium.org>

committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 28 14:51:21 2026
tree
f13eaea404695e5bffdaf813680517d4f234bb87
parent
7554590a7a0b7e065448182716513043c471931d [ diff ] 
Use SameDomainOrHost in CrossOriginResourcePolicy::ShouldAllowSameSite

In CrossOriginResourcePolicy::ShouldAllowSameSite, origins were
previously compared using GetDomainAndRegistry. For origins with a null
registrable domain (such as single-label hostnames, public suffixes, or
opaque origins), GetDomainAndRegistry returns an empty string for both,
causing distinct hosts lacking a registrable domain to be treated as
matching.

This CL replaces the local GetDomain check with
net::registry_controlled_domains::SameDomainOrHost. This ensures that
origins without a registrable domain must have identical hostnames to
be considered same-site, matching the WHATWG Fetch and HTML
specifications.

TAG=agy

[Extensions] Wait for listeners before dispatching runtime.onEnabled
📋 Metadata
commit
312fad7d68bd8038c6d6b527dad649d1cbc5c982
[ log ]
author
Justin Lulejian <jlulejian@chromium.org>
Wed Oct 28 14:48:09 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 28 14:48:09 2026
tree
be4abe196176a2ff50a54893e2652bb014673c01
parent
57f055a2c7cce88a4e358d691cace020d6408d31 [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Wait for listeners before dispatching runtime.onEnabled

Installing or updating an extension clears its persisted event
listeners. When an extension was installed or updated while it was
disabled, enabling it dispatched `runtime.onEnabled` without waiting for
the background context. The extension's service worker had not yet run
its top-level script to register its listeners again, so the event was
dropped. `runtime.onInstalled` and `runtime.onExtensionLoaded` were
delivered in these cases, because they wait for the background context.

After this change, `runtime.onEnabled` is delivered in these cases by
waiting for the service worker's top-level script to register its
listeners. A plain enable still dispatches `runtime.onEnabled`
immediately.

Signal --single-argument via elevation service to isolated browser
📋 Metadata
commit
5ccc9f2c90cf738707c46b78a012519a05683e81
[ log ]
author
Will Harris <wfh@chromium.org>
Wed Oct 28 14:43:27 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 28 14:43:27 2026
tree
1906302d658edbab41704989146a0206d10ae874
parent
637c6f9f795e0098164161d5650ff52261c78ca1 [ diff ] 
Signal --single-argument via elevation service to isolated browser

--single-argument can't be passed through elevation service as it might
allow the injection of undesirable arguments.

However, it is used for metrics to determine the type of launch, as it's
used in the shell "open" registry keys.

If the elevation service sees this argument, it adds
--source-single-argument to indicate that the shell launched Chrome this
way, and the existing logic that unbundles and verifies command line
switches continues to handle this command line by appending it fully
after the -- marker and removes the --single-argument switch.

This ensures that the signal that the shell has launched chrome is
maintained, without allowing arbitrary argument injection.

1c06461e311042155d710a9d63c19a0447bd9cc9 [ diff ] 
[Signin][Android] Reauth primary account before secondary for ADDSESSION

If the target account is healthy but the primary account is in error
state,start the reauth flow for the primary account and then redirect to
the continue_url. If the target account itself is in error (including
when both accounts are), the behavior is unchanged.

The new behavior is guarded by the kAddSessionReauthPrimaryAccountFirst
kill switch (enabled by default).

tree
a7635ae36dd7042186c3415f1813d0a10e57840c
parent
65073dfdbea472f67bd12d2f7dd92b2ee78264c8 [ diff ] 
[infra] Remove telemetry_perf_unittests from Android x64 emulator FYI bots

android-17-x64-fyi-rel and android-canary-x64-fyi-rel pick up
telemetry_perf_unittests_android_chrome from the shared
android_fyi_isolated_scripts bundle, but neither builder has the
telemetry dependencies, so the suite fails every time.

Adding chromium_with_telemetry_dependencies would make gclient syncs
slower and make the builders harder to reproduce locally. It also
wouldn't help much: the system_health smoke stories, which are the main
reason the suite needs those dependencies, can't run on x64 emulators
because there is no clear_system_cache binary for android_x86_64
(b/578069352).

Remove the suite from these two FYI builders instead. Coverage remains
on android-10-x86-rel.

Bug: 578069352, 524833517

WebGL-on-WebGPU: Implement Vertex Array Objects (VAO)

Implement createVertexArray, deleteVertexArray, and bindVertexArray in
WebGLRenderingContextWebGPUBase.

Query GL_MAX_VERTEX_ATTRIBS and create the default VAO wrapper at
context initialization. Track ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER and
vertexAttribPointer buffer attachments on the currently bound VAO so
bound buffers have their lifetime extended and are unbound on
deleteBuffer.

Revert CHECK to DCHECK for IsUsingDefaultName in FinalizeNewProfileSetup

In release builds, entry->IsUsingDefaultName() can return false under
certain profile setup flows, leading to a fatal CHECK failure in
FinalizeNewProfileSetup.

This change reverts the CHECK back to a DCHECK until it can be safely
converted once we are confident it will not be triggered.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/fdaa75bb08400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

[iOS] Re-enable landscape fullscreen sign-in promo test

Historically, the fullscreen sign-in promo was prevented from appearing
in landscape mode on iPhone (crrev.com/c/3999908, crrev.com/c/4000305).
Support for iPad in landscape was later introduced in crrev.com/c/4538883,
but the test was disabled in crrev.com/c/4540209 due to flakiness
(crbug.com/40266894).

With the modern two-screens sign-in flow based on PromoStyleViewController
(using UIModalPresentationFormSheet), the fullscreen sign-in promo now
properly supports landscape orientation on both iPhone and iPad.

This CL re-enables the test as `testSignInPromoInLandscapeMode`,
verifying that the promo is displayed and sufficiently visible when
foregrounding the app in landscape mode across both phone and tablet form
factors.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

🌺 Disable SearchPreloadUnifiedBrowserTest.PrerenderHintReceivedAfterSucceed on Android

The following test failed all retry attempts (hard-failed) in 3/3
consecutive builds on chromium/ci/android-14-tablet-landscape-arm64-rel
(builds #11629, #11630, and #11631).

Disabled here:
  SearchPreloadUnifiedBrowserTest.PrerenderHintReceivedAfterSucceed (Android only)

Example failing runs:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-14-tablet-landscape-arm64-rel/11631
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-14-tablet-landscape-arm64-rel/11630
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-14-tablet-landscape-arm64-rel/11629

Why disable rather than revert:
Fails deterministically at content/public/test/prerender_test_util.cc:237
with PrerenderFinalStatus::kActivationNavigationParameterMismatch (50)
instead of PrerenderFinalStatus::kActivated (0) with no single culprit
commit in the regression range (sibling tests
PrerenderHintReceivedBeforeSucceed and PrerenderHintReceivedAfterCompletion
in the same file are already disabled on #if BUILDFLAG(IS_ANDROID)).

To run/deflake this test on CQ before re-enabling, add the following
footer(s) to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.android:android-14-tablet-landscape-arm64-rel|android_browsertests

This is mitigation, not a fix. The bug tracks the real investigation
and is assigned to the owning team.

[iOS][Actor] Run ActorService commands in call order

Enqueues `PerformActions`, `RequestTabObservation`, `InterruptTask`,
`StopTask` and `StopAllTasks` so task logic runs in call order as posted
tasks, never on the caller's stack, including from observer and delegate
callbacks. Getters only reflect a command once it has run, e.g. a
stopped task stays active until its stop runs.

Replies are always asynchronous. Pending commands, replies and lifecycle
notifications are dropped on `Shutdown()`, after which `GetWeakPtr()` is
null.

a46cdf6362a33f20cbbef0b384d609f1a39589f8 [ diff ] 
[payments] Expose test constants in WebView PaymentRequest tests

PaymentRequestTestWebPageContents and MockAndroidIntentLauncher (in
//components/payments/) hardcode HTML button IDs and mock payment
response values that the WebView PaymentRequest tests (in
//android_webview/) repeat as raw string literals. Because these values
are duplicated across separate targets instead of shared via constants,
changing the test helpers in //components/payments/ would silently break
the WebView tests.

Expose public static final String constants on
PaymentRequestTestWebPageContents and MockAndroidIntentLauncher and
reference them across AwPaymentRequestServiceTest,
AwPaymentRequestSettingsTest, and AwPaymentRequestDefaultSettingsTest.

[Sync] Coordinate attachment uploads in processor

This CL introduces `AttachmentUploader` and integrates starting and
resuming attachment uploads into `ClientTagBasedDataTypeProcessor`.
Entities with pending attachment uploads are held back from commit while
their session URL is persisted in `EntityMetadata`, and committed once
the upload completes with a `temporary_blob_id`.

Design doc (Googlers only):
go/blobsync-upload-dd

Implementation plan (Googlers only):
go/blobsync-upload-plan

tree
7210c21f84fe37d71729c5836dab6fa9119ade32
parent
f18e93b96e3ad132950847a8cba82d0a3c68d424 [ diff ] 
[iOS][TTC] Defer page context extraction until session is live

Previously, TtcSessionController started observing active tab changes
and extracting page context immediately during StartSession(), under the
assumption that outgoing frames would be queued safely by the backend.
However, this allowed ContextUpdate frames to be dispatched or queued
ahead of SessionSetupRequest and ToolsetUpdate, violating backend
handshake sequencing.

Defer starting VoiceFocusedWebStateTracker observation and initial page
context extraction until OnConversationInitialized(), after the session
transitions to TTCSessionLifecycle::kLive and toolset definitions have
been sent. Additionally, ignore voice-focused tab changes and fetched
page context callbacks unless the session lifecycle is strictly kLive.

[agents] Ground Core Scanner in chrome_browser_design_principles.md

Update the multi-agent-code-review Core Scanner persona
(personas/core/auditor.json) and SKILL.md instructions to explicitly
check //chrome/browser architectural design principles:
- Enforce narrow constructor and member dependency injection (e.g.
  accepting and storing PrefService* rather than retaining coarse
  god-objects like Browser*, BrowserProcess*, Profile*, or
  BrowserWindowInterface* merely to traverse to a sub-property).
- Verify feature controller scoping across GlobalFeatures,
  ProfileKeyedService, BrowserWindowFeatures, and TabFeatures.
- Disallow chrome::FindBrowserWithTab / BrowserList in modularized
  feature code and check fallible API return values before recording
  side effects.

audio: Fix WASAPIAudioInputStream buffer sizing and FIFO bounds

Compute converter input block sizes in
SetupConverterAndStoreFormatInfo() using exact 64-bit integer math and
clamp sub-frame upsampling block sizes to at least 1 frame.

Gate delivery in PullCaptureDataAndPushToSink() on buffering at least
min_blocks_for_convert_ blocks (computed via
AudioConverter::GetMaxInputFramesRequested()) while continuing to drain
queued packets via GetNextPacketSize(), and reset/re-prime converter_ in
Stop().

Also zero-initialize allocated blocks in IncreaseCapacity() and use
release CHECK bounds checks in AudioBlockFifo.

All tests succeeded locally on real audio hardware.

parent
95702587691dc0edb8003d34a46c3a42dafa0253 [ diff ] 
[aw] Only relax prerender param matching for browser navigations

ShouldAllowPartialParamMismatchOfPrerender2() is intended to relax
prerender activation matching for navigations triggered by
WebView.loadUrl(). It identified such navigations solely by the
PAGE_TRANSITION_FROM_API qualifier on the page transition.

WebView.loadUrl() always issues a browser-initiated navigation, so
additionally require that the activation navigation is
browser-initiated. This avoids relaxing matching for renderer-initiated
navigations that happen to carry the qualifier, where the strict
initiator and header comparisons should continue to apply.

tree
c9535f2f3ae1d8f4f28149b19eb991047da95110
parent
45e129c924a1e26fab4ed5138479b5ee3a9af0a1 [ diff ] 
[AdTracker] Fix monkey-patch attribution for Proxies with apply traps

Problem:
The monkey-patch heuristic misattributes the true caller when a Proxy
patch with an `apply` trap wraps another patch:
  1. Non-Ad Caller -> Ad Patch (via Proxy) -> Non-Ad Patch -> Native API
  2. Ad Caller -> Non-Ad Patch (via Proxy) -> Ad Patch -> Native API
In these scenarios, the call is attributed to the Proxy patch instead of
the caller, i.e. a false positive in (1) and a false negative in (2).

Cause:
The heuristic takes the caller of the patched API as the true caller.
For a Proxy, it matches both the `apply` trap and the target. Matching
the target is meant for Proxies without a trap, which call the target
directly. However, with a trap, the target is called by the trap and is
matched first, so the trap is mistakenly taken as the true caller.

Solution:
Match a Proxy's target only if the Proxy has no `apply` trap, so that
only the outermost patch (the trap, if any) is matched.

Reland "Use -mcmodel=medium for ELF x64 sanitizer builds"

This is a reland of commit 4714cff9778c641f4d0ef4ce958a9142a8488b2c

Excluded `is_ubsan_security` builds from `-mcmodel=medium` because
`-fsanitize=function` is only supported with `-mcmodel=small`.

Original change's description:
> Use -mcmodel=medium for ELF x64 sanitizer builds
>
> Sanitizer instrumentation inflates data sections (.data, .bss, etc.),
> making large binaries prone to exceeding the 2GiB displacement limit of
> the default small code model and failing at link time with "relocation
> R_X86_64_PC32 out of range".
>
> On Windows (PE/COFF) and macOS (Mach-O), the object formats do not
> support large sections, so we only enable the flag for ELF targets.
>

Wed Oct 28 14:02:54 2026
tree
b6154eafa1ec64f33130347ffdbf34cbb162b6ae
parent
32d740788efa9be4a385071149b4297e8b5eb8cc [ diff ] 
FSA: Block ~/.cache on all platforms in sensitive path blocklist

GenerateBlockPaths() only blocked ~/.cache via base::DIR_CACHE under
IS_LINUX, IS_CHROMEOS, and IS_ANDROID. On macOS, base::DIR_CACHE
resolves to ~/Library/Caches, and on Windows base::DIR_CACHE does not
exist, leaving ~/.cache unblocked under base::DIR_HOME
(kDontBlockChildren) despite cross-platform tools storing virtual
environments, package caches, and tokens there. On Linux and ChromeOS,
~/.cache was also left unblocked when $XDG_CACHE_HOME pointed
elsewhere, or for secondary signed-in profiles on ChromeOS.

Add a platform-independent BlockPath::CreateRelative(base::DIR_HOME,
".cache", kBlockAllChildren) rule alongside ~/.config, and keep the
base::DIR_CACHE rule on Linux, ChromeOS, and Android to cover custom
$XDG_CACHE_HOME locations and Android's app cache directory.

[Views] Guard live menu updates against malicious AX callbacks

Use the new ViewGuard class to prevent AX code from synchronously
closing a menu that is being actively updated.

The previous ViewTracker was insufficient as there were calls whose
contents were not themselves protected; this approach is more
heavy-handed but will avoid any deletion of the menu under the call.

tree
7dbe2c7fc0f8ee063e9e73270aaa5c1b9d393f97
parent
48ed48514ea068e88981113e97db5e8b65694433 [ diff ] 
ash: Expand touch_point_view_ width to fit tap_label_

In TouchCalibratorView::InitViewContents(), tap_label_ was updated to
use its preferred width to accommodate longer localized "Tap here"
strings, with its x-offset clamped to 0 via std::max(..., 0). However,
its parent container touch_point_view_ still had a fixed width of
kTapLabelWidth (80px), and throbber_circle_ was positioned assuming that
fixed width. When a localized tap_label_ exceeded kTapLabelWidth,
touch_point_view_ clipped the label and left it horizontally misaligned
with throbber_circle_.

Size touch_point_view_'s width to at least tap_label_'s preferred width
(std::max(kTapLabelWidth, preferred_label_size.width())), center both
throbber_circle_ and tap_label_ horizontally within touch_point_view_,
position hint_box_view_ relative to throbber_circle_ rather than
touch_point_view_->width(), and compute GetDisplayPointLocation() using
throbber_circle_'s center point rather than
touch_point_view_->width() / 2.f.

Screencast: http://screen/xFFGQuHt6h5ytwN
Before: http://screen/9KZcV8s7ryn6vkX
After: http://screen/AqGoMvVmCLMSb3X

Android: Use nullaway.jar from the error_prone CIPD package

NullAway is now packaged within the error_prone CIPD package, so that
it stays in sync with Error Prone (and its native-image). This deletes
//third_party/android_build_tools/nullaway.

This updates NullAway from 0.14.2 to 0.14.3, which flags unboxing of
@Nullable values in more places. Fixes the 4 new warnings.

[iOS] Re-enable and stabilize contextual panel EG2 tests

Re-enable all 10 `ContextualPanelTestCase` EarlGrey tests in
`ios/chrome/browser/contextual_panel/ui/contextual_panel_egtest.mm`,
which were previously blanket-disabled on the iOS simulator by the
gardener in https://crrev.com/c/7933369 under `crbug.com/523259471` due
to test flakiness.

The tests flaked with "Failed waiting for web state to be visible" when
calling `[ChromeEarlGrey loadURL:...]`. In
`testCloseLastTabWithPanelOpen` and
`testCloseLastTabBeforeLargeEntrypointAppears`, closing the active tab
at index 0 left the tab count at 0. On iPhone, closing the last tab
transitions Chrome to the Tab Grid. Because `ContextualPanelTestCase`
configures `config.relaunch_policy = ForceRelaunchByCleanShutdown`,
Chrome cleanly shut down and serialized its session while sitting on the
Tab Grid. Upon relaunch for subsequent tests, Chrome restored directly
to the Tab Grid without an active web state, causing subsequent
`[ChromeEarlGrey loadURL:...]` calls to time out waiting for a visible
web state.

Stabilize both tests by waiting for
`[ChromeEarlGrey waitForMainTabCount:0]` and then explicitly restoring
the browser to an active web state via
`chrome_test_util::TabGridNewTabButton()`, `waitForMainTabCount:1`, and
loading `/defaultresponse` before the test completes. Ensure `setUp`
opens a new tab if `mainTabCount` is 0. For
`testContexutalPaneliPadMultiwindow`, preserve the iOS 26+ multi-window
skip under `crbug.com/427699033` using `@available(iOS 26.0, *)`.

Verified by running all 10 tests across multiple iterations on
iPhone 17 Pro Max (iOS 27.0) (2x iterations, 20/20 passed) and iPad Pro
11-inch (iOS 26.5) (10/10 passed).

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

[iOS] Fix flake in testSubmissionCountReporting_UnloadPage

In full_address_form.html, when triggerMultipleSubmissionsNoProgrammatic
is enabled, clicking #submit-button synchronously dispatches only the
first submission (submitCount = 1) and chains the 3 additional button
click submissions asynchronously via setTimeout(..., 10).

Because [ChromeEarlGrey tapWebStateElementWithID:@"submit-button"]
returns as soon as the synchronous click() evaluation finishes,
immediately calling [ChromeEarlGrey reload] races against the pending
setTimeout callbacks and can unload the document before all 4
submissions have been dispatched.

Wait for submitCount === 4 before reloading the page so that all 4
submissions are recorded prior to the unload report.

TAG=agy
CONV=22fa7f08-5017-4569-9bbe-179670de85dd

[enterprise] Deflake ProfileManagementDisclaimerService focus test

Passively attach the disclaimer completion callback before replacing the
browser window in
ProfileManagementDisclaimerServiceBrowserFocusBrowserTest.

In ProfileManagementDisclaimerServiceBrowserFocusBrowserTest.Test,
ReplaceCurrentBrowserWithNewOne() activates the new browser and triggers
OnBrowserActivated (kEnterpriseManagementDisclaimerAfterBrowserFocus).
CloseBrowserSynchronously() pumps a nested message loop that completes
the cancel flow and removes the account before the test calls
EnsureManagedProfileForAccount(). Calling
EnsureManagedProfileForAccount() afterward starts a redundant second
flow on an untracked account, stalling 5 seconds on
extended_account_info_wait_timeout before resetting with false.

Add ProfileManagementDisclaimerService::AddCallbackForTesting() to
register the TestFuture callback before
ReplaceCurrentBrowserWithNewOne(), and remove the redundant
post-replacement EnsureManagedProfileForAccount() call. Synchronize all
variants (including Dismiss and NotManaged) on future.Wait(), remove
base::RunLoop().RunUntilIdle(), and verify the
expected_profile_creation_required_by_policy callback argument.

Evidence:
* ProfileManagementDisclaimerServiceBrowserFocusBrowserTest.Test/
  Managed_EnforcedByPolicy_Cancel stalled 5 seconds and failed 20/20
  runs without the fix when asserting required_by_policy, receiving
  false instead of true.
* With the fix, all 9 parameter variants pass deterministically in 13 s
  total, and Managed_EnforcedByPolicy_Cancel runtime drops from ~10.5 s
  to ~6.0 s without stalling.
* Coverage check: A deliberate mutation in
  OnManagedProfileCreationResult storing false causes the
  EnforcedByPolicy parameter variants to fail at the required_by_policy
  assertion.
* Related tests: All 6 ProfileManagementDisclaimerServiceBrowserTest.*
  tests continue to pass.

Re-enable Managed_EnforcedByPolicy_Cancel in
testing/buildbot/filters/accessibility-linux.browser_tests.filter.

The test and the fix were written with AI assistance.

[UnboundedElement] Dismiss macOS surface on clicks outside window

On macOS, UnboundedSurfaceWindowMac is a borderless NSWindow
without an event monitor, so mouse clicks on browser UI outside
the active web contents (such as the tab strip) never reached the
active RenderWidgetHostImpl.

Install a local NSEvent mouse-down monitor on
UnboundedSurfaceWindowMac for left, right, and other mouse-down
events to dismiss the surface when the user clicks outside the
unbounded window.

[iOS] Introduce ChromeConnectionOptions

Wraps UISceneConnectionOptions, which cannot be instantiated outside
UIKit, in a Chrome-owned ChromeConnectionOptions object. SceneDelegate
gets a -scene:willConnectToSession:chromeOptions: entry point, used by
the UIKit callback and by the scene reconnection in MainController, so
the cold-start connection flow can be driven by options not created by
UIKit.

[policy] Add presubmit check for policy YAML files

Validate modified policy YAML files and translatable Grit messages in
presubmit.

crrev.com/c/7123104 landed invalid YAML syntax and placeholders in
policy definitions that bypassed CQ and broke downstream Grit builds. An
earlier presubmit check (crrev.com/c/7877278) was reverted because it
unconditionally injected data['name'] into all YAML files, causing
_AddMessages() to fail with a TypeError on messages.yaml.
CheckYamlFormat now validates affected policy templates using
ValidateForPresubmit, restricting data['name'] injection strictly to
individual policy definitions, unpacking policy_atomic_groups.yaml
captions, and skipping deleted or non-dict files.

Tests and validation:
* Added PolicyJsonUnittest.testValidateForPresubmit in
  tools/grit/grit/gather/policy_json_unittest.py covering valid
  policies/messages, invalid XML, and conflicting placeholders.
* Added CheckYamlFormatTest in components/policy/PRESUBMIT_test.py
  covering valid policies, messages.yaml, atomic groups, deleted files,
  syntax errors, invalid XML, conflicting placeholders, and unrelated
  files.
* Without the fix, PolicyJsonUnittest and CheckYamlFormatTest fail with
  AttributeError due to missing attributes. With the fix, all tests in
  tools/grit/grit/gather/policy_json_unittest.py (13/13) and
  components/policy/PRESUBMIT_test.py (25/25) pass hermetically.
* Verified test coverage with deliberate-break patches: commenting out
  ValidateForPresubmit or omitting messages assignment reliably triggers
  test assertion failures.
* git cl presubmit reports 0 errors and 0 warnings.

The presubmit check, Grit gatherer method, and unit tests were written
with AI assistance.

[iOS] Convert FullscreenJSFeature to use ScriptMessageValue

ScriptMessageValue was introduced to reduce the latency of messages
passed from the JS process to the browser process.

This CL replaces this JavaScriptFeature's use of the legacy base::Value
stored message with the new ScriptMessageValue class.

2dab07ecc74ce855ff908e47745420aa13a1fdeb [ diff ] 
[UndoTabClosures] Add TabLaunchType FROM_UNDO_CLOSURE

Adds a launch type for tabs restored by the undo-closure snackbar. Such
tabs are created in the background at the index the caller chooses and
are never selected by the tab model; the restore logic handles
selection. It is a background launch that skips the creation animation,
keeps the requested insertion index, maps to the "UndoClosure" histogram
variant and is persisted as value 37. willOpenInForeground and
willAddedTabBeSelected always return false for it, even when the current
model's incognito state differs.

66879e271490eaac9dc9c545d6c7262530daf726
parent
51de1aad9a337fd7a7e45bac037a5338baec4ab2 [ diff ] 
[Net][CPRS] Fix request state and lifetime on dynamic routing updates

1. Two-stage request pause tracking (`override_rules_evaluated_`):
   Requests can pause either before ProxyOverrideRules (POR) evaluation
   or after POR evaluation completes without matching. Track
   `override_rules_evaluated_` so resuming a post-POR paused request
   starts directly at ContinueProxyResolution() (PvD + PAC) without
   re-probing POR DNS, and reset it on full config resets.

2. Dynamic routing and PAC state fixes:
   - Issue #1 (request lifetime and in-flight jobs):
     - Guard the pending request resume loop in OnProxyConfigChanged()
       with a WeakPtr in case a synchronous callback destroys `this`.
     - Cancel in-flight per-request PAC jobs (`resolve_job_`) on PvD
       updates so they re-evaluate updated routes without hitting
       CHECK(!is_started()), while leaving in-flight POR DNS probes
       running.
   - Issue #2 (concurrent PAC init and PvD updates):
     - Check `!IsPacReady()` in OnProxyConfigChanged() so PvD-only
       updates during PAC init update `fetched_config_` in-place
       without restarting PAC fetch.
     - Keep pending requests paused when PAC init finishes while a PvD
       update is still in progress, and transition them back to
       WAITING_FOR_INIT_PAC on full config resets.

parent
32da8f3a5dfca63a0a47f14829af6295a113b936 [ diff ] 
ttc: Skip the actor observation delay for TTC actions

TTC doesn't take an observation after performing an action; page context
is delivered separately by TtcPageContextMonitor. Waiting for the page
to settle (ObservationDelayController) therefore only delays the tool
response to the model, by up to 10s on busy pages.

Add ToolRequest::SetSkipObservationDelay(), which makes
actor::ToolController complete a tool as soon as it finishes invoking
without creating an ObservationDelayController. Follow-up actions
enqueued by a tool (e.g. OpenUrlTool's NavigateTool) inherit the flag
from the action that enqueued them. TTC sets it on every action it
performs.

01954031a1325e3dd2018648680013213d69fc49 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Fix and re-enable CredentialProviderServiceTest.TwoStores

Fixes flakiness in `CredentialProviderServiceTest.TwoStores` (disabled
on physical devices in crrev.com/c/8606052) and re-enables the test.

In crrev.com/c/7807438, `AddCredentialsRefactored()` moved both the
favicon freshness scan (`GetFaviconsListAndFreshness()`) and the
`MemoryCredentialStore` additions (`[store addCredential:credential]`)
into an asynchronous `base::ThreadPool` callback, whereas
`AddCredentialsLegacy()` added credentials synchronously on the main
sequence. Because `RemoveCredentials()` remained synchronous on the main
sequence, a credential removal could run before an earlier
`AddCredentialsRefactored()` task finished, allowing the delayed add to
re-insert a removed credential (or causing `TwoStores` to observe only
one store's addition before the subsequent removal).

Update `AddCredentialsRefactored()` to add credentials to `store` and
invoke `completion` (`SyncStore()`) synchronously on the main sequence,
in the same order as removals, and only offload
`GetFaviconsListAndFreshness()` to `base::ThreadPool` with a new
`FetchStaleFavicons()` reply callback (since favicon fetches only write
favicon files on disk without touching the credential stores).

In `CredentialProviderServiceTest`, re-enable `TwoStores` on all
platforms and call `task_environment_.RunUntilIdle()` in the
`AddCredentialsRefactored_*` tests that assert on `favicon_loader_` so
that the deferred `GetFaviconsListAndFreshness()` task completes before
checking favicon fetch expectations.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

6d5b749f3ffcd41c5cfcef3222a1461e2de4db78
parent
9e088c5b8e7cf3193a8bb09a7e0782be63a30c6e [ diff ] 
[VGS] Track the Settings process by handle rather than pid (1/6)

See: go/vgs-with-ui-automation-api

SettingsWindowFinderWin now keeps the process handle it validates a
candidate window's image path through, opened with
PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION | SYNCHRONIZE, and hands it to the
owner with the HWND. VisualGuidedSetterControllerWin holds that
base::Process instead of a bare pid: while held, Windows does not
reuse the pid, and IsRunning() (a zero-timeout wait) tells whether the
window can still be vouched for. IsValidSettingsProcess() now requires a
running, tracked process whose pid owns the HWND.

This is the identity the upcoming UI Automation probe will re-check
before every query, so that a reuse HWND value can never point it at
another window.

b56168737e54e84dd687eb3590a12c1fa14c46f4 [ diff ] 
[Android] Ensure first frame finishes drawing before starting native init

During cold startup, Chrome waits for the Activity's first UI frame to
be drawn before kicking off heavy C++ browser initialization
(onCreateWithNative), so that the initial window appears on screen as
quickly as possible (measured by
Startup.Android.Cold.TimeToFirstFrame2).

However, a behavior in AsyncInitializationActivity allowed native
initialization to run *before* Android actually finished drawing the
first frame:

 * Running native init synchronously inside draw callbacks:
   Android invokes ViewTreeObserver's onPreDraw() and onDraw() callbacks
   *before* ViewRootImpl hands the frame off to RenderThread to be
   rendered. Running firstDrawComplete() synchronously inside those
   callbacks blocked the frame handoff until onCreateWithNative()
   finished.

This CL fixes these issues by:
- Posting firstDrawComplete() to the UI thread task queue in
  AsyncInitializationActivity#onFirstDrawComplete(), allowing the
  current frame traversal to finish handing the frame to RenderThread
  before native initialization begins.

No functional behavior change is expected.

[VerticalTabs] Remove reflection from vertical tab unit tests

Vertical tab Robolectric tests previously used ReflectionHelpers to
mutate LayoutParams span indices, stub unattached ViewHolder adapter
positions and view types, and read internal coordinator drag listeners.
This coupled tests to private field names and bypassed real RecyclerView
binding.

Drive real SimpleRecyclerViewAdapter layouts, View#dispatchDragEvent
with DragEventBuilder, and observable layout/item-decoration checks
instead, requiring zero production code changes.

ios: Ensure installed system frameworks are writable in codesign.py

Ensure files copied by `InstallSystemFramework` in `codesign.py` are
user-writable (`CopyWritable`) and pass `dirs_exist_ok=True` to
`shutil.copytree` so read-only SDK files (such as in Cog or CIPD
checkouts) do not fail with `PermissionError` on incremental builds.

c772d815d86043cac0e5ee68e094d403204d0975 [ diff ] 
[WebRTC] Re-enable remote log multi-profile unit tests

Re-enables
`WebRtcEventLogManagerTest.RemoteLogFileCreatedInCorrectDirectory` and
`WebRtcEventLogManagerTest.LogMultipleActiveRemoteLogsDifferentBrowserContexts`
after fixing file cleanup, renderer process host references, and upload
suppression.

In crrev.com/c/2742999 and crrev.com/c/2768521,
`WebRtcEventLogManagerTest.RemoteLogFileCreatedInCorrectDirectory`
was disabled due to flakiness. The test created active remote logs
across multiple `TestingProfile` directories but never invoked
`OnPeerConnectionRemoved` before test exit. As a result, the
`TestingProfile` destructors deleted temporary profile directories
while background log file handles could still be open on the task
runner. Calling `OnPeerConnectionRemoved` on each peer connection
ensures all remote log files are safely closed and flushed before the
profiles are destroyed.

In crrev.com/c/2323863 and crrev.com/c/2805725,
`WebRtcEventLogManagerTest.LogMultipleActiveRemoteLogsDifferentBrowserContexts`
was disabled due to flakiness. The test also mistakenly referenced
`rph_->GetID().value()` (the test fixture's default
`MockRenderProcessHost`) instead of `rphs[i]->GetID().value()` when
constructing distinct log payloads for each profile's peer connection.
Correcting this reference ensures each profile's log entries match its
respective renderer process.

Furthermore, once all peer connections are removed in these multi-
profile tests, completed remote log files become eligible for upload
and schedule a background upload task. If delayed upload execution
fires during or across test runs, subsequent profile destruction cancels
the unexpected upload and triggers test failures in
`NullWebRtcEventLogUploader::Cancel()`. Temporarily setting the network
connection type to `net::NetworkChangeNotifier::CONNECTION_NONE` via
`base::ScopedClosureRunner` suppresses background upload scheduling
during both tests.

Both tests now run reliably. Added documentation comments explaining
the scenario, preconditions, and expectations for both unit tests.

Verified with 20/20 passes locally using `--gtest_repeat=20`.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

Fixed: 40752893, 40709493

[iOS] Add AppLaunchConfirmationRequestCause::kOpenAppStoreFromIncognito

Add a dedicated
`AppLaunchConfirmationRequestCause::kOpenAppStoreFromIncognito` overlay
configuration and string (`IDS_IOS_OPEN_APP_STORE_FROM_INCOGNITO`) to
prompt the user when a site attempts to open the App Store from
Incognito.

This change also postpones the expiry of the associated histogram
`Tab.ExternalApplicationOpened{Cause}` to after 2027-04-26.

9629c0ffd25968b70ca40f84520484148a27ee65
parent
7948cd45d99edfd1f95c42150c168e835ba3f30f [ diff ] 
[Autofill] Remove unused AddressComponent::ConsumeAdditionalToken()

AddressComponent::ConsumeAdditionalToken() has no callers or overrides
in the codebase, and its fallback branch for non-atomic components
erroneously concatenated the parent component's GetValue() instead of
subcomponents_[0]->GetValue(). Remove the unused method.

5f3119f5c38d113528d9aecddca1afda3ca768ba [ diff ] 
private_insights: Remove TensorFlow runner factory workaround

FCP used to crash during eligibility eval when no TensorFlow runner
factory was registered, so Chrome registered a dummy factory returning
nullptr. This has been fixed upstream in FCP (d88c8103, "Handle missing
TensorflowRunner gracefully in eligibility eval"), which is included in
the currently rolled revision, so the workaround is no longer needed.

Test: manual E2E upload against prod FCP server
Bug: b:528431754

tree
89c022e5859805b437caeb66eec69474a9f788bd
parent
6923080ad31365eee532886a1c77baea198fac43 [ diff ] 
Avoid self-aliasing key erasures in net/http

- In `BrokenAlternativeServices::OnDefaultNetworkChanged()`, move
  `broken_alternative_services_on_default_network_` into a temporary via
  `std::exchange()` before iterating and calling `Confirm()`, so
  `Confirm()` does not erase the `std::set` element referenced by its
  `const BrokenAlternativeService&` parameter.
- In `HttpServerProperties::RemoveAltSvcCanonicalHost()`, pass the
  `canonical` iterator directly to `canonical_alt_svc_map_.erase()`
  instead of `canonical->first`, avoiding both a redundant lookup and
  passing a reference into the `base::flat_map` slot being erased.

Fixed: 578570282

5bcb0ba1097f5d600a37f888b53d6949ffd2fe87 [ diff ] 
[Predictors] Fix and re-enable PrefetchManagerTest.StopAndStart

PrefetchManagerTest.StopAndStart was flaky across platforms due to two
bugs in the test and FakePrefetchManagerDelegate:

1. `requests` appended `/should_be_cancelled` as the (limit + 1)-th
   request so the initial `Start()` followed by `Stop()` could verify
   that queued and coalesced requests are not sent. However, the
   subsequent `Start(main_frame_url, requests)` call (which restarts
   prefetching after the stopped PrefetchInfo finishes and does not call
   `Stop()`) reused the same `requests` vector still containing
   `/should_be_cancelled`. Once `responses2[0]` finished,
   PrefetchManager launched the queued `/should_be_cancelled` request,
   tripping the request monitor's
   `EXPECT_NE(request.relative_url, "/should_be_cancelled")` check
   whenever the request reached EmbeddedTestServer before teardown.
2. `FakePrefetchManagerDelegate::WaitForPrefetchFinished(url)` never
   removed `url` from `finished_urls_`, so the second
   `WaitForPrefetchFinished(main_frame_url)` call returned immediately
   instead of waiting for the restarted prefetch batch to finish, making
   the `/should_be_cancelled` failure a race with test teardown.

Remove `/should_be_cancelled` from `requests` before restarting the
unstopped prefetch batch, erase `url` from `finished_urls_` in
`WaitForPrefetchFinished(url)`, verify `expected_prefetch_requests` on
the restarted batch, and re-enable `PrefetchManagerTest.StopAndStart`.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

[iOS] Re-enable ThroughputAnalyzerTest unit tests

Re-enable ThroughputAnalyzerTest.MaximumRequests,
ThroughputAnalyzerTest.MaximumRequestsWithNetworkAnonymizationKey, and
ThroughputAnalyzerTest.TestThroughputWithMultipleRequestsOverlap on iOS,
which were disabled for flakiness in crrev.com/c/2311116.

Two underlying issues have been resolved since crrev.com/c/2311116:
1. `ThroughputAnalyzer::MaybeGetThroughputObservation()` previously
   used `static_cast<int64_t>(std::ceil(downstream_kbps_double))`,
   causing undefined float-to-integer overflow on ARM64 when elapsed
   window durations were near zero and resulting in negative throughput
   values that dropped valid observations. This was fixed with
   `base::ClampCeil()` in crrev.com/c/5390944.
2. Unlike the other throughput tests in throughput_analyzer_unittest.cc
   (such as TestMinRequestsForThroughputSample and
   TestThroughputWithNetworkRequestsOverlap), these three tests use the
   wall-clock DefaultTickClock without calling
   `SetStartTimeNullHttpRtt(base::Seconds(100))`. With the default
   115 ms HTTP RTT, `EraseHangingRequests()` drops in-flight requests
   and ends the observation window if creating 1,000 requests or waiting
   for request completion takes >= 3 seconds under bot load. Extract a
   `SetLargeHttpRtt()` helper to set HTTP RTT to 100 seconds across the
   throughput tests, preventing false hanging-request timeouts and
   allowing these three tests to be re-enabled on iOS (keeping
   MaximumRequests disabled on Android for crbug.com/1223950).

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

c912e9f1f671129636986f0c86b4c252cc4cab28

committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 28 12:09:02 2026
tree
b5132a40e0e4581c4015ef050269064fe4f60bb0
parent
01e23755876a315b92134b425f60a892c1c91834 [ diff ] 
[Signin] Avoid AvatarToolbarButton animations and state listeners when hidden

On ChromeOS regular profiles, AvatarToolbarButton is created in
ToolbarView::Init() and hidden because
AvatarToolbarButtonInterface::CanShowForProfile() returns false.
However, state transitions (such as OnSigninCoordinator collapsing
after 20s) still triggered slide_animation_ and
highlight_color_animation_ on the hidden button, scheduling no-op
compositor frames via label()->layer() and regressing
TPS.Display.Smoothness in idle performance tests.

Fix this by:
1. Only creating NormalStateProvider in
   AvatarToolbarButtonStateManager::CreateStatesAndListeners() when
   !AvatarToolbarButtonInterface::CanShowForProfile(profile).
2. Only calling label()->SetPaintToLayer() when
   AvatarToolbarButtonInterface::CanShowForProfile() is true.
3. Skipping slide_animation_ in AvatarToolbarButton::AnimateTextChange()
   when !GetVisible().
4. Skipping highlight_color_animation_ in
   ToolbarButton::HighlightColorAnimation::Show()/Hide() when
   !parent_->GetVisible().

tree
7f4043ea00f48b09dfe55fee8c28175da76711a1
parent
73806fe15873ce60edd4a7e5aa0c0d950666670c [ diff ] 
[Chrome Android] Clip the first feed card to the containment's rounded top.

FeedItemDecoration draws the containment's 24dp rounded top behind the
first card, but nothing clips the card to it. When that card's image
covers the entire container, its media paints over the curve and its
corners show as square. The clip follows whichever card is first, so
dismiss and undo are covered, and it's removed from views that get
recycled. It only applies to the single-column layout while the feed
header is hidden.

Behind the new FeedContainmentClipFirstCard feature (disabled by
default), clip the first card to an outline that rounds only its top
corners, using home_surface_ui_background_radius so it matches the
containment.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
[iOS] Avoid redundant topInset and trait updates in BVC & BrowserLayout
📋 Metadata
commit
f6f6b781f70ddb88a36832e4e8b5774233d891ba
[ log ]
author
Scott Yoder <scottyoder@google.com>
Wed Oct 28 11:45:08 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 28 11:45:08 2026
tree
2b547825e4d48cc9164f5088200f490e0a3e59ed
parent
0905e60ad5304e1ac2b5b9275aa28b62433c5831 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Avoid redundant topInset and trait updates in BVC & BrowserLayout

During background scene snapshots, UIKit overrides appearance traits
(such as UITraitUserInterfaceStyle and UITraitSceneCaptureState).
Previously, BrowserViewController, BrowserLayoutViewController, and
BrowserLayoutCoordinator registered for trait changes via
TraitCollectionSetForTraits(), which registers all 18 mutable traits
while kEnableTraitCollectionRegistration is disabled. This caused their
trait handlers to run on every snapshot trait flip and contribute to
0x8BADF00D scene-update watchdog timeouts under CPU starvation.

- Register BrowserViewController, BrowserLayoutViewController, and
  BrowserLayoutCoordinator directly for UITraitVerticalSizeClass and
  UITraitHorizontalSizeClass using selector-based actions
  (-sizeClassDidChange / -sizeClassDidChange:previousTraitCollection:).
- Only query -[BrowserLayoutViewController topInset] (which walks
  SafeAreaProvider and SceneState.window) when the tab strip can be
  shown, and reuse the resolved topInset across layout updates.
- Reuse secondaryToolbarHeightWithInset in
  -[BrowserViewController sizeClassDidChange:previousTraitCollection:].

Add JavaScriptModificationType::kSubstringCompletion

In address pickers, sometime users write substrings of the options they
want to select instead of a full prefix:
(E.g. "York" --> "New York", "Amphithe" --> "1600 Amphitheater Pkwy").

This CL adds a JavaScriptModificationType::kSubstringCompletion enum
value so that single-field fillers are detected even in such user
journeys.

[iOS] Re-enable testContextMenuWithDisabledEditOption

Re-enable DISABLED_testContextMenuWithDisabledEditOption in
ManagedBookmarksPolicyDataWithSubFolderTestCase, and extract string
literals in managed_bookmarks_egtest.mm into constexpr NSString*
constants in the anonymous namespace.

Previously, two issues caused this test to fail:

1. BookmarkEarlGreyUI dismissContextMenu tapped the center of
   grey_keyWindow() instead of using ChromeEarlGreyUI
   dismissContextMenuIfPresent and waiting for
   _UIContextMenuContainerView to disappear. Furthermore, when EarlGrey
   injects a long press on a UITableViewCell to present a context menu,
   UIScrollViewDelayedTouchesBeganGestureRecognizer calls
   -[UITableView touchesBegan:withEvent:] (setting
   _pendingSelectionIndexPath to the long-pressed row), and presenting
   _UIContextMenuContainerView detaches the cell before
   GREYTouchInjector delivers UITouchPhaseEnded. As a result,
   UITableView never receives touchesEnded/touchesCancelled and leaves
   _pendingSelectionIndexPath set to First_Managed_URL, causing the
   subsequent tap on Managed_Sub_Folder to select First_Managed_URL and
   dismiss Bookmarks. Fix this by clearing _pendingSelectionIndexPath
   via BookmarkEarlGreyAppInterface cancelBookmarksTableViewTouches in
   dismissContextMenu.
2. openCustomManagedBookmarksFolder and openCustomManagedSubFolder did
   not wait for the pushed folder's contents to appear after tapping the
   folder row, allowing the subsequent long press to race with the
   navigation push transition. Wait for the expected child node to
   appear before returning.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

be3c4d2cbbddba5138eef33e1c87316ad61dfef9 [ diff ] 
Improve IsPossibleMouseClickTargetElement()

Two discoveries made during testing:
- <button> defined without type="button" default to having
  FormControlType::kButtonSubmit as per the HTML standard.
- Many frameworks use various ARIA role attributes to represent
  comboboxes and their corresponding options.

This CL allows kButtonSubmit buttons without an explicitly specified
type="submit", and also relaxes the condition of the element having
role="option" to any of the elements ancestors having an ARIA role
attribute among a list of found ones.

Additionally, the CL adds an extra restriction that the click target
should not be a <form> element or a blur click in <html> or <body> as a
protective layer against some noise.

Use initial and final value to compute JavaScriptModificationType

Some dropdowns clear values in the fields before setting them to the
actual final values (E.g. "Cal" --> "" --> "California").

In order to make our algorithm independent of those intermediate step,
this CL modifies the logic that used to only consider the first write
operation, to start comparing the first initial value with the value of
the field at the time of DetectJavaScriptAutofill().

This fixes single-field fillers where we require having
JavaScriptModificationType::kPrefixCompletion.

The working assumption here is that the behavior post-CL is at least as
good as the one pre-CL.

📋 Metadata

72711b96c8731da99178a13e8e68b4b318e4362c
parent
36513c4dac23eb18adcd1fe86a77b9d0086e83a0 [ diff ] 
Defer scheduled restart for one idle threshold after system wake

With "Restart when idle" scheduled, closing a laptop lid and opening it
later could relaunch Chrome within one idle poll of the wake, before
the user had touched the machine. The restart is now deferred until a
full idle threshold of awake time has passed since the wake.

The cause is that platform idle clocks in ui/base/idle/ handle sleep
differently. On Windows and Linux/Wayland the reported idle time
includes time spent asleep, so the first poll after a wake already
exceeds the threshold. macOS and Linux/X11 exclude the sleep but carry
pre-sleep idle time across the wake, so the restart can still land
shortly after the user returns. In every case the first post-wake
reading says nothing about how long the user has been away from a
running machine.

While monitoring for idle restarts, ScheduledRestartManager detects
sleep and wake with PowerSuspendObserver. Because OS power
notifications are best-effort, it also infers sleep from wall-clock
vs. base::LiveTicks skew between idle polls. Skew alone is not enough
on Windows Modern Standby, where base::LiveTicks can keep advancing
in standby.

A missed OnResume() does not block the restart indefinitely: a sleep
detected via skew, or user input after OnSuspend(), also clears the
suspended state.

A deferral caused by a recent wake is recorded as
ScheduledRestartBlocker::kRecentSystemWake in
Session.ScheduledRestart.BlockerEncountered.

2259a9b70a5fe1468aa0e32c0bed2176441ad006 [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Add isolated_mode extension pref

Previously, an extension's off-the-record setting was always stored in
the "incognito" pref, so it carried over between Incognito mode and
Enterprise Isolated Mode.

This change adds a separate "isolated_mode" pref with dedicated
ExtensionPrefs::IsIsolatedModeEnabled() and
ExtensionPrefs::SetIsIsolatedModeEnabled() accessors.
util::IsIncognitoEnabled() and util::SetIsIncognitoEnabled() now pick
between the Incognito and Isolated Mode prefs based on a new
ExtensionsBrowserClient::IsolatedModeReplacesIncognito(), while
ExtensionPrefs uses the active mode internally to keep
ExtensionPrefValueMap consistent.

IsolatedModeReplacesIncognito() is lazily cached in ExtensionPrefs on
first use because some tests enable Isolated Mode after ExtensionPrefs
is created. Once those tests are updated, it can be initialized in the
constructor (crbug.com/576933996).

Sync changes will come in follow-up CL.

DD: go/force-install-isolated-mode-dd

[download] Pre-reserve OfflineItem vector in RunGetAllItemsCallback

Problem:
DownloadOfflineContentProvider::RunGetAllItemsCallback ->
std::__Cr::__uninitialized_allocator_relocate<OfflineItem> during vector
reallocations in items.push_back() represents 0.25% of browser main
thread jank:
http://pprof/?id=9761aa380e0077276ea92eb799cc5a86&filter=focus:std%253A%253A__Cr%253A%253A__uninitialized_allocator_relocate&pivot=DownloadOfflineContentProvider::RunGetAllItemsCallback&flamesearch=push_back

Solution:
Behind the kPreReserveOfflineItemVector feature (disabled by default),
call items.reserve(all_items.size()) before populating items in
DownloadOfflineContentProvider::RunGetAllItemsCallback(). Even if
ShouldShowDownloadItem() filters out some items, over-allocation is
temporary as items is destroyed right after callback runs.

[download] Optimize overwritten download path counting in DownloadHistory

Problem:
Inside DownloadHistory::LoadHistoryDownloads, ShouldSkipLoadingDownload
represents 0.1% of browser main thread jank:
http://pprof/?id=9761aa380e0077276ea92eb799cc5a86&filter=focus:ShouldSkipLoading&pivot=DownloadHistory::LoadHistoryDownloads

CountFilePathOccurences() and ShouldSkipLoadingDownload() convert every
row.target_path to UTF-8 via AsUTF8Unsafe() twice, allocate a
std::string per row, and insert/lookup in a std::map<std::string, int>.

Solution:
Key file_name_count by base::FilePath::StringViewType in an
absl::flat_hash_map (reserved to rows.size()) referencing
row.target_path.value() directly without UTF-8 conversion or string
allocation.

6f6ef1af0b257df5cba13d124e2498d66255beee
parent
230a3c32d33bd639adf6c11756a0add3955e09aa [ diff ] 
Avoid shutdown hangs during system log upload

SystemLogUploaderDelegate loads and compresses system log files on
base::ThreadPool. Previously, these background tasks omitted an explicit
TaskShutdownBehavior, inheriting SKIP_ON_SHUTDOWN. Under
SKIP_ON_SHUTDOWN, any task already in-flight when shutdown begins blocks
the UI thread in ThreadPoolInstance::Shutdown(). Because reading and
redacting large log files can take dozens of seconds, watchdog timers
fired and produced shutdown hang crash reports in
RedactionTool::RedactCustomPatternWithContext.

This CL adds CONTINUE_ON_SHUTDOWN to LoadSystemLogs and ZipSystemLogs so
in-flight background log reads do not block browser termination.

Bug: b:570429694

The test was originally disabled under https://crbug.com/40458377
with a note stating that history was not yet marked as a filterable
datatype. However, `TimeBasedHistoryRemoval` tests time-scoped history
deletion using
`content::BrowsingDataRemover::RemoveWithFilterAndReply()` with an empty
`BrowsingDataFilterBuilder::Mode::kPreserve` filter.
`filter_builder->MatchesAllOriginsAndDomains()` evaluates to `true`
for an empty preserve filter, and
`ChromeBrowsingDataRemoverDelegate::RemoveEmbedderData()` explicitly
allows non-filterable datatypes when `MatchesAllOriginsAndDomains()` is
`true`. The test verifies that deleting history in a specified time
window (`AnHourAgo()` to `base::Time::Max()`) correctly deletes entries
within that window while preserving older history entries.

Tested by re-enabling and running
`ChromeBrowsingDataRemoverDelegateTest.TimeBasedHistoryRemoval`
10/10 times locally in `unit_tests` without failure.

Part of https://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

[Sync] Clean up unreachable Nigori key derivation failure handling

`Nigori::CreateByDerivation()` is guaranteed to return non-null and
`Nigori::GetKeyName()` is guaranteed to return a non-empty string.
Propagate these guarantees to callers by removing unreachable failure
checks in `KeystoreKeysCryptographer::FromKeystoreKeys()` (and its
callers), `CustomPassphraseSetter::TryApply()`, and
`RequiredPassphraseVerifierImpl::IsValidDecryptionPassphrase()`, and
updating the corresponding method comments.

[Passwords] Mock IsReauthBeforeFillingRequired on automotive in PhishGuard test

With kDeviceReauthRefactor enabled, AllPasswordsBottomSheetController
checks PasswordManagerClient::IsReauthBeforeFillingRequired() before
requesting a DeviceAuthenticator. Because MockPasswordManagerClient
returns false by default for IsReauthBeforeFillingRequired(),
GetDeviceAuthenticator() was not called in
OnCredentialSelectedTriggersPhishGuard on automotive builds.

db12d22d7532ac257db956f922c752d48ec2d8b6 [ diff ] 
[BrowsingData] Re-enable `DomainReliability_NoMonitor` in `unit_tests`

Re-enables
`ChromeBrowsingDataRemoverDelegateTest.DomainReliability_NoMonitor` in
`chrome/browser/browsing_data/chrome_browsing_data_remover_delegate_unittest.cc`.

This test was disabled under https://crbug.com/40458377 because the original
test fixture unconditionally created a `DomainReliabilityMonitor`,
preventing it from verifying browsing data removal when no monitor was
installed.

Domain Reliability has since been migrated out of profile-level
monitors into the Network Service via
`network::mojom::NetworkContext::ClearDomainReliability()`. When
removing `constants::DATA_TYPE_HISTORY` and
`content::BrowsingDataRemover::DATA_TYPE_COOKIES` without installing a
`ClearDomainReliabilityTester` override,
`ChromeBrowsingDataRemoverDelegate` invokes its default
`domain_reliability_clearer_` callback to clear data through the
storage partition's network context. In `unit_tests`, `TestingProfile`
provides the default network context, allowing the test to verify that
clearing completes cleanly without crashing or hanging.

Verified by running 10/10 consecutive passes of
`ChromeBrowsingDataRemoverDelegateTest.DomainReliability_NoMonitor`
locally in `unit_tests`.

Part of https://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

media: Switch VoiceIsolation tests to the stateful test model

Switch LoadVoiceIsolationTestModel() to load the stateful test model
(test_model_stateful_1_2_160_2.tflite) by default now that
TfLiteVoiceIsolation resets TFLite resource variable state after the
warmup invocation and in ClearBuffers(), and remove the now-redundant
LoadStatefulVoiceIsolationTestModel() helper.

For tests that require a stateless model with known
frequency-independent per-channel gains
(SpeechMaskMatchesStatelessModelDenoisingOnlyGain,
KeepsHighBandWithStatelessModel, and
OutputIsInputDelayedByAlgorithmicDelayWithStatelessModel), add
BuildStatelessTestModel() to build an in-memory affine FlatBuffer model
with distinct dereverberated and denoising-only gains and non-zero
output biases.
